फा०मा० 2462. —-इसके द्वारा , लेख्य प्रमाणक नियम ( नोटेरीज 
रूल्स ) , 1956 के नियम 6 के अनुसार, सक्षम प्राधिकारी द्वारा सूचना 
दी जाती है कि उक्त प्राधिकारी को श्री भगवान पाजभवामी , एउघोकेट 
सिंध चोक श्री पुरा कोटा ने उक्त नियमों के नियम 4 के अधीन , फोटा 
में लेख्य प्रमाणक ( नोटेरी ) का काम करने की नियुक्ति के लिए आवेदम 
पत्र भेजा है । 

उक्त व्यक्ति की लेख्य प्रमाणक के रूप में नियुक्ति के बारे में यषि 
कोई आपत्तियों हो तो वे इस नोटिस के प्रकाशित होने के चौदह दिन 
के अन्दर नीचे हस्ताक्षर करने वालों को लिख कर भेज दिये जाये ।

( 1961 का 43) की धारा 10 की उपधारा ( 23-ग ) के खण्ड ( v )

जहाँ कोई प्रायफर सर्किल , वार्ड या जिला या उसका माग इस 
पधिसूचना द्वारा एक रेंज से किसी अन्य रेंज को अन्तरित हो जाता है, 
यहाँ उस प्राय- कर सकिल वार्ड या जिले या उसके भाग में किए गए 
निर्धारणों से उत्पन्न होने वाली और उस रेंज के , जिससे यह प्राय -फर 
सफिल , वार्ड या जिला या उसका भाग अन्तरित हुआ हैं , सहायक प्राय -कर 
पायुक्त ( मपील ) के समक्ष इस अधिसूचना की तारीख के ठीक पूर्व 
लम्बित अपीलें उस तारीख से जिस तारीख को यह अधिसूचना प्रभावी 
होती है , उस रेंज के जिसको उक्त सकिल , बाई या जिला या उसका 
भाग मन्तरित हुमा है , सहायक प्रायकर प्रायुक्त ( पपील ) को अन्तरित 
की जाएगी और उसके द्वारा उन पर कार्यवाही की जाएगी । । 
यह मधिसूचना 24-10- 77 से प्रभावी होगी । 

[ सं० 20 25/फा० सं० 261/ 23 / 77 - पाई टी घे ]

जहाँ कोई आयकर सकिल , वार्ड या जिला या उसका भाग इस 
अधिसूचना द्वारा एक रेंज से किसी अन्य रेंज को अनार हो जाता है , 
वहाँ उस प्राय -कर सकिल वार्य या जिले या उसके भा । में किए गए 
निर्धारणों से उत्पन्न होने वाली और उम रेंज के , जिससे वह पाय-कर 
सकिल, वाई या जिला या उसका भाग अन्तरित हुआ है , सहायक माय -फर 
प्रायफ्त ( अपील ) के समक्ष इस अधिसूचना की तारीख के ठीक पूर्व 
लम्बित अपीलें उस तारीख से जिस सारोख को यह अधिसूचना प्रभात्री 
होती है, उस रेंज के , जिमको उक्त सकिल , पार्ड या जिल या उसका भाग 
अन्तरित हुपा है सहायक प्रायकर प्रायुक्त ( अपील ) को अन्तरित की जाएगी 
और उसके द्वारा उन पर कार्यवाही की जाएगी । 
यह अधिसूचना 1- 12- 1977 से प्रभावी होगी ।

Whore an Income-tax Circle , Ward and Distt . or part there 
of stands transferred by this notification from one range to 
another range appeals arising out of assessments made in that 
Income- tax Circle, Ward or district or part thereof and pending 
immodiately before the date of this notification before the 
appellate Asstt . Commissioner of Income-tax from whom that 
Income-tax Circle , Ward or district or part therof is transferred 
shall from the date this notification shall take effect be trans 
ferred to and dealt with by the Appellato Asstt. Commissioner 
of Income-tax of the range to whom the said circlo , ward or 
district or part thereof is transferred . 

Where all circle , wards or districts having headquarters at 
a particular place have been assigned to an Appellate Assistant 
Commissioner, he will have jurisdiction in respect of circles , 
wards and districts at these hoadquartors since abolished also .

S . O . 2489 .- - In exercise of the powers conferred by sub - sec 
tion (1 ) of Section 122 of the Income-tax Act , 1961 (43 of 1961 ) 
and of all other powers enabling it in that behalf and in super 
session of all the previous directions on the subject the Central 
Board of Direct Taxes hereby directs that tho Appellite Assistant 
Commissioner of Income -tax , C . Range, Madras shall perform 
all his functions in respect of the persons falling within the juris 
diction of the Income-tax Officers undermenioned :

जहां कोई मायकर मकिल , वाई या जिला या उसका भाग इस 
अधिसूचना द्वारा एक रेंज से किसी अन्य रेंज को अन्तरित हो जाता है , 
वहां उस आय-कर सकिल , वाई या जिने या उसके भाग में किए गए 
मिर्धारणों से उत्पन्न होने वाली और उस रेंज के , जिससे वह प्राय-कर 
सकिल , वाई या जिला या उसका भाग मन्तरित हुमा है , सहायक प्राय 
कर आयुक्त ( अपील ) के समक्ष इस अधिसूचना की तारीख के ठीक पूर्व 
लम्बित अपीलें उस तारीख से जिस तारीख को यह अधिसूचना प्रभाषी 
होती है, उस रेंज के, जिसको उक्त सकिल, वार्ड या जिला या उसका भाग 
अन्तरित हुभा है सहायक आयकर प्रायुक्त ( अपील ) को अन्तरित की जाएगी 
और उसके द्वारा उन पर कार्यवाही की जाएगी । 
यह अधिसूचना 1-1-78 से प्रभावी होगी । 

[ सं० 2086 ( फा० सं० 261/ 6 / 77-प्राईटी जे ) ]

the Appellato Assistant Coinmissioner of the Range from when

transferred to and dealt with by the Appellate Assistant Commis 
sioner of the Range to whcm the said Circle , Ward or District 
or part thereof is transferred .

S . O . 2491. - In exercise of the powers conferred by sub 

ion ( 1 ) of Section 122 of the Income - tax Act. 1961 ( 43 
of 1961 ) and all other powers enabling it in that behalf and 
in partial modification of the previous Notification No. 1760 
( F . No. 261 / 20 / 77- ITJ) dated the 7th May , 1977 , the 
Central Board of Direct Taxes , hereby directs that the 
following amendments may be made : 
( 1 ) in the jurisdiction of the AAC, KR, Kolhapur viz . 

aftor entry No. 17 in Col. No. 2 add " No, 18 , 

C - Ward , Ichalkaranji " 3B 
( 2 ) in the jurisdiction of the AAC, TR, Thena viz . 

after entry No. 44 in Col No . 2 add " No. 45 , 

Z -Ward , Puno " . 
This notification shall take effect from 2- 1- 1978 . 

[ No. 2091 ( F . No . 261/ 20 /77-ITJ) ] 
नई विस्ली , 6 जनवरी , 1978 


जहां कोई प्राय - फर सकिल, बार्ड या जिला या उसका भाग इस 
अधिसूचना द्वारा एक रेंज से किसी अन्य रेंज को अन्तरित हो जाता है , 
यहां उस आय -कर सकिल, वार्ड या जिले या उसके भाग में किए गए 
निर्धारणों से उत्पन्न होने वाली और उस रेंज के , जिससे वह माय -कर 
सकिल , वाई या जिला या उसका भाग अन्तरित हुआ है , सहायक प्राय -कर 
पायुक्त ( अपील ) के समक्ष उस अधिसूचना की तारीख के ठीक पूर्व 
मंषित अपील उस तारीख से जिस तारीख को यह अधिसूचना प्रभावी 
होती है , उस रेंज के , जिसको उक्त सकिल , वार्ड या जिला या उसका 
भाग अन्तरित हुआ है सहायक प्राय -फर प्रायुक्त ( अपील ) को अन्तरित 
की जाएगी और उसके द्वारा उन पर कार्यवाही की जाएगी । 
यह अधिसूचना 6 - 1-1978 से प्रभावी होगी । 

[ सं० 2110 ( फा० सं० 261 / 2/ 77- आई टी जे )] 


New Delhi, the 6th January , 1978

. जहाँ कोई प्रायकर सकिल , वार्ड या जिला या उसका भाग इस अधि 
सूचना द्वारा एक रेंज से किसी अन्य रेंज को अन्तरित हो जाती है, वहां 
उस पाय -कर सकिल बार्ड या जिले या उसके भाग में किए गए निर्धारणों 
से उत्पन्न होने वाली और उस रेंज के , जिससे वह आय -कर सर्किल , 
वाई या जिला या उसका भाग अन्तरित हुआ है , सहायक माय -कर आयुक्त 
( अपील ) के समक्ष इस अधिसूचना की तारीख के ठीक पूर्व लंबित 
अपीलें उस तारीख से जिस तारीख को यह अधिसूचना प्रभावी होती है, 
उम रेंज के, जिसको उक्त सकिल , वार्ड या जिला या उसका भाग 
अन्तरित हुपा है सहायक प्रायकर प्रायुक्त ( अपील ) को अन्तरित की जाएगी 

और उसके द्वारा उन पर कार्यवाही की जाएगी । 
___ यह अधिसूचना-18-1-1978 से प्रभावी होगी ।

stands transferred by this Notification from one Range to another 
Range , appeals arising out of the assessinents made in that

का० प्रा० 2 496. -- प्राय -कर अधिनियम , 1961 ( 1961 का 43 ) की 
धारा 12 2 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों और इस निमित्त उसे 
समर्थ अनाने वाली अन्य सभी शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए और इस संबंध 
में अधिसूचना सं० 2109 ( फा० सं० 261/ 8 / 77- आई० टी० जे० ) 
तारीख 5- 1 -1978 का प्रांशिक उपांतरण करते हुए, केन्द्रीय प्रत्यक्ष कर 
घोर्ड निवेश देता है कि नीचे की अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 2 में विनिर्दिष्ट रेंजों के 
महायक प्रायकर आयुक्त ( अपील ) उसके स्तम्भ 3 में तत्सम्बन्धी प्रविष्टि

में विनिर्दिष्ट प्रायकर मकिलों, वागे पौर जिलों में प्रायकर या अधिकार से उक्त सकिल, वार्ड या जिला या उसका भाग अन्तरित हुपा है सहायक 
निर्धारित सभी व्यक्तियों और प्रायों के बारे में अपने कृत्यों का पालन करेंगे : - - प्रायकर मायुक्त ( अपील ) को अन्तरित की जाएगी और उमफे धारा उन

का० प्रा० 2521. - भारत सरकार की अधिसूचना के द्वारा जैसा कि 
यहाँ संलग्न अनुसूची में प्रदर्शित किया गया है और पेट्रोलियम और खनिज 
पाईप लाइन ( प्रयोगकर्ता के भूमि अधिग्रहण प्रधिकार ) अधिनियम , 1962 
के सण्ड 6 के उपखण्ड ( 1 ) के अन्तर्गत प्रकाशित किया गया है, गुजरात 
राज्य के खंभात तेल क्षेत्र में उक्त परिशिष्ट भूमि में वेघान स्थल सं० 
23 से जी० जी० एस० तक पेट्रोलियम के लिए भूमि उपयोग के अधिकार 
प्राप्त किए गए हैं । 

तेल एवं प्राकृतिक गैस मायोग में उपर्युक्त नियम के बण 7 के 
उपरड ( 1 ) की धारा ( i ) में निविष्ट कार्य दिनांक 20- 7- 1976 से 
समाप्त कर दिया गया है । 

प्रतः प्रम पेट्रोलियम पाइप लाइन के नियम ( प्रयोगकर्ता के भूमि 
अधिग्रहण पधिकार ) नियम, 1983 के अन्तर्गत सक्षम प्राधिकारी एतब् 
पारा उक्त तिथि को कार्य समाप्ति की तिपि अधिसूचित करते हैं । 


मई दिल्ली , 10 अगस्त, 1978 
का० प्रा० 25 2 2 :-- - पतः इस संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट और 
पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकारों का 
प्रजन ) अधिनियम , 1962 की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन 
प्रकाशित भारत सरकार की अधिसूचना द्वारा इण्डियम पायल कार्पोरेशन 
लिमिटेस के लिए गुजरात राज्य के सलाया से उत्तर प्रदेश में मथुरा तक 

और गुजरात राज्य में विरमगाम से गुजरात शोधनशाला कोयली तक 
पेट्रोलियम के परिवहन के लिए उस संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों 
के उपयोग का अधिकार मर्जित कर लिया गया है । 
___ और यत : इण्डियन प्रायल कार्पोरेशन लिमिटेड में उक्त अधिनियम 
की धारा 7 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के खण्ड ( 1 ) में निर्दिष्ट प्रक्रिया को 
अनुसूची में निर्विष्ट गांव के नाम के सामने दिखाई गई तिथि से पर्यवसित 
कर दिया है । 

प्रम प्रतः पेट्रोलियम पोर पनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि के उपयोग के 
अधिकारों का प्रजन ) नियमावली, 1963 के नियम 9 के अधीन , सक्षम 
प्राधिकारी उक्त तिथि को उपर निर्दिष्ट संक्रिया पर्यवसान के रूप में 
एतद्वारा अधिसूचित करते है ।

ऊर्जा मंत्रालय 

( कोयला विभाग ) 

नई दिल्ली , 14 अगस्त , 1978 
का०मा० 2525.- -केन्द्रीय सरकार ने कोयला वाले क्षेत्र ( मर्जन मोर 
विकास ) अधिनियम , 1957 ( 1957 का 20 ) की धारा 7 की उपधारा 
( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकर के ऊर्जा मंत्रालय ( कोयला विभाग) की 
अधिसूचना सं० का०मा० 3556, तारीख 23 सितम्बर , 1976 
धारा उस मधिसूचना से उपाबन्ध अनुसूची में वणित भूमि में से खनिजों 
के खनन , खदान वेधन , खोदाई, तलाश निष्कासन उन पर कार्य करने 
पौर उनको ठुलाई करने के अधिकारों के प्रर्जन के भाशय की सूचना 
वी पी ; 

पूर्वोक्त क्षेत्रों में खनन के अधिकारों के अधिग्रहण के बारे में कोई 
माक्षेप नहीं किए गए हैं ; 

केन्द्रीय सरकार का , उड़ीसा सरकार से परामर्श करने के पश्चात् 
यह समाधान हो गया है कि इससे उपाबद अनुसूची में वर्णित 629 . 00 
एकड़ ( लगभग ) या 25 4 . 54 हेक्टर ( लगभग ) माप की भूमि में 
खनिजों के खनन , खदान , बंधन , खोदाई , तलाश , निष्कासन , उन पर कार्य 
करने और उनकी डुलाई करने के अधिकारों का अर्जन किया जाना चाहिए । 

प्रतः प्रज , केन्द्रीय सरकार, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 9 की उपधारा 
( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए यह घोषणा - करती है कि 
इससे उपाबन्ध अनुसूची में वर्णित 629 . 00 एकर ( लगभग ) पा 25 4 . 54 
हेक्टर ( लगभग ) माप की भूमि में खनिजों के खनन , खवान धन , 
खोदाई , तलाश, निष्कासन, उन पर कार्य करने और उनकी दुलाई करने 
के अधिकारों का अधिग्रहण किया जाता है । 

इस अधिसूचना के अन्तर्गत पाने वाले मन्त्र के रेखांक का निरीक्षण , 
कलक्टर के कार्यालय सम्बलपुर उड़ीसा में या कोयला नियंत्रक के कार्यलय 
1 काउंसिल हाउस स्ट्रीट कलकता में या वेस्टर्न कोलफील्ड्स लिमिटेड 
( राजस्व अनुभाग ) का कार्यालय विसेसर हाउस, टेम्पल रोड, नागपुर 
( महाराष्ट्र ) में किया जा सकता है ।

MINISTRY OF ENERGY 

(Department of Coal) 

New Delhi, 14th August , 1978 . 
S . O . 2525 . Whereas by the notification of the Govern 
ment of India in the Ministry of Energy (Department of Coal) 
S . O . No . 3556 dated the 23rd September, 1976 under sub 
section ( 1) of section 7 of the Coal Bearing Areas (Acquisition 
and Development Act), 1957 (20 of 1957) , the Central Govern 
ment gave notice of its intention to acquire the rights to mine , 
quarry , bore, dig and search for, win , work and carry away 
minerals in the lands described in schedule appended to that 
notification ; 


And whereas no objection was made to the acquisition of 
mining rights in the locality aforesaid ; 

And whereas the Contral Government after consulting the 
Government of Orissa , is satisfied that the rights to mine, quarry , 
bore , dig and search for, win , work and carry away mincrals in 
the lands mesuring 629. 00 acres (approximately ) and or 234 .54 
hectares (approximately ) described in Schedule appended hereto 
should be acquired . 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the powers conferred by sub 
goction ( 1 ) of Section 9 of the said Act , the Central Government 
hereby declares that the rights to mine, quarry , bore , dig and 
Search for, win , work and carry away minerals in the lands 
measuring 629 .00 acres (approximately ) or 254 . 54 hoctares 
(approximately) described in the schedule appended hereto aro 
hereby acquired . 

The plans of tho area covered by this notification may 
ba inspected in the Office of the Colloctor , Sambalpur 
(Orissa ) or in tho Ofico of tho Coal Controller, 1 , Council 
Horiso Stroot, Calcutta , or in tho Office of the Western 
Coalfields Limited (Revənue Section ), Bisosor House Temple 
Road , Nagpur (Maharashtra ).

प्रोद्योगिक प्रधिकरण, विल्ली की सेवाएं उनकी सेवा निवृत्ति के कारण 
अब उपलब्ध नहीं रही हैं और केन्द्रीय सरकार उम्त विवाद को न्याय 
निर्णय के लिए हाल में गठित केन्द्रीय सरकार प्रोद्योगिक प्रधिकरण , नई 
दिल्ली , को स्थानान्तरित करना वांछनीय समानती है ।

विवाद से सम्बद्ध कार्यवाही को वापस लेती है और उसे उक्त अधिनियम

का०मा० 2567 - - यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है 
कि मैसर्स वि परफेक्शन हाउस , करोलबाग , अजमल खां रोड, नई दिल्ली , 
नामक स्थापन से सम्बद्ध नियोजक और कर्मचारियों की बहुसंख्या इस 
बात पर सहमत हो गई है कि कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि और प्रकोण 
उपबन्ध अधिनियम , 1952 ( 1952 का 10 ) के उपबन्ध उक्त स्थापन 
को लागू किए जाने चाहिए ; 

प्रतः प्रब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 1 की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा 
प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त अधिनियम के 
उपबन्ध उक्त स्थापन को लागू करती है । 
यह अधिसूचना 1 नवम्बर, 1977 को प्रवृत्त हुई समझी जाएगी ।

का०मा० 2568. - - यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है 
कि मैसर्स कोटपाड लार्ज साइज्ड मल्टीपरपस कोमापरेटिव सोसाइटी 
लिमिटेड , डाकघर कोटपाउ , जिला कोरापुट ( उड़ीसा ) , नामक स्थापन 
से सम्बर नियोजक और कर्मषारियों की बहुसंख्या इस बाप्त पर सहमत 
हो गई है कि कर्मचारी भविष्य निधि और प्रकीर्ण उपबन्ध अधिनियम , 
1952 ( 1952 का 19 ) के उपबन्ध उक्त स्थापन को लागू किए 
जाने चाहिए ।

BEFORE SHRI THIRU K . SELVARATNAM , B . A . , B . L ., 

INDUSTRIAL TRIBUNAL , MADRAS 
( Constituted by the Central Government) 
Monday , the 31st day of July , 1978 

Industrial Dispute No . 2 of 1978 
(In the matter of the dispute for adjudication under Sec 
tion 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of the Industrial Disputes Act, 1947 between 
the workmen and the Management of Life Insurance Corpo 
ration of India, Madurai. ) 

BETWEEN 
The workmen represented by The President, Insurance 

Corporation Employees Union , Madurai Division , 
Post Box No . 2 , Madurai-625001. 

AND 
The Divisiemal Manager, Lifc Insurance Corporation of 

India , Sellur , Madurai-625002. 
REFERINE : 


Order No . 1 - 17012 ( 2 ) / 77 - D . IV ( A ), dated 21st January , 

1978 of the Ministry of Labour , Government of 

Indii. 
This dispute coming on this day for final hearing upon 
perusing the refercnce , claim and counter statements and all 
other material papers on record and upon hearing the argu 
ments of Thiru N . G . R . Prasad , Advocate for Thiruvalargal 
K . Chandru and R . Rajuram , Adyocate appearing for the 
workmen and of Thiru D . Ramamurti, Advocate for the 
Management, this Tribunal made the following 

AWARD 
This is an Industrial Dispute under Section 10 ( 1 )(d ) of the 
Industrial Disputes Act , 1947 between the workmen and 
the Management of Life Insurance Corporation of India , 
Madurai and their workmen in respect of grant of special 
increments to two persons, viz ., Thiruvalargal Ganapathy, 
Assistant, Divisionul Office , Madurai and Iyyemperumal, Assis 
tant, Branch Office, Tuticorin . 

( 2) The following is the reference : 
· Whether the management of LIC of India are justified 

in denying the grant of special increments to 
(i) Shri Ganapathy, Assistant, Divivional Officer , 
Madurai- II and ( ii ) Shri Jyyompcrumal, Assistant, 
Branch Office , Tuticorin ? If not, to what relief are 

the concerned workmen entitled ? 
( 3 ) A Claim Statement was filed by thc Union , wherein they 
state as follows : Thiruvalargal S . Ganapathy and E . Iyamperu 
mal, Assistants were working in Madurai Divisional Office 
and Tuticorin Branch Office respectively and they were ap 
pointed on 23rd October, 1970 . They have to pass the Asso 
cialeship Examination of the Federation of Insurance Insti 
tute for earning special incremonts . There are several groups 
of subiccts left to the option of the candidate and the candl 
date would be deemed to have passed the Associateship Exa 
mination if he passes any one of such groups of subjects . 
They have to sit for the examination within five years and 
pass the subjects to earn increment. The Associateship Exami 
nation of the Federation of Innurance Institute was commenc 
ed on 20th October, 1975. As per the Time- Schedule for the 
subjects as prescribed by the Federation of Insurance Institute 
they sat for the examination for thcir subjects on 23rd Octo 
ber, 1975 and passed the test . But the Management denied the 
special increment on the grounds that on 23rd October, 1975 
tho day on which they wrote the first examination subject 
they had co oleted five years of service on 22nd October , 
1975, the previous day. Nowhere it is stipulated in the Cen 
tral Office Circular that the employees should have completed 
all the papers before the prescribed time limit of five years 
to become eligible for the award of special increment. The 
Associateship Examination of Federation of Insurance Insti 
tute was commenced on 20th October, 1975 i . c., thrce days 
carlier to the completion of five years of service and the 
fixing of the time schedule was beyond their control and 
jurisdiction of the candidates . A subsequent Central Office 
circular clarified the position by stating that an employee 
should not have completed five years or ten years of service E19 
the case may be, as on the 1st day of the commencement of 
the examination , on the passing of which he becomes eligible 
to special increment . Therefore it is fair and proper that the 
benefit of this explanation should go to Thiruvalargal S . Gana 


pathy and E . Iyemperumal and a special increment is made 
available to them with effect from the due dates , Therefore 
the Tribunal may be pleased to direct the Respondent-Manage 
ment to award increments by passing the Associateship Exami 
nation with effect from 1st January , 1976 and direct the 
Management to pay the amounts to the employees . 

(4 ) A counter statement was filed by the Life Insurance 
Corporation of India , wherein they contend as follows : In the 
ycar 1959 the Corporation decided to introduce a scheme for 
grant of special increments to those employees who pass 
certain prescribed technical examinations. As per ihe Scheme 
introduced these cmployces were to pass the prescribed techni 
cal examinations within a period of five years from the date 
of joining or before 30th of June , 1962 , whichever is later , 
Later, there were some amendments by way of administrative 
instructions . As a result of the negotiation between the Man 
agement and their workmen under a Settlement entered into , 
it was agreed that the employees who were in the services 
of the Corporation on 1st April , 1969 will be granted special 
increments if they pass the prescribed technical cxamination 
by 31st of December , 1975 . It way also agreed that they 
should sit for the examination within the period of five years 
and they will be eligible for the increments even if the results 
come after the expiry of the period of five years . In as much 
as the petitioners are concerned they had not sat for the 
examination within the ylipulated period of five ycars, they 
will not be entitled to special increment claimed by them and 
their claim is liable to be dismissed . 

( 5 ) ISSUE : It is the common ground that the Petitioners 
entered service in the Life Insurance Corporation of India 
As Assiytants on 23rd October , 1970 . Ex . W - 1 is the Central 
Office Circular dated 30th August , 1975 and it contemplates 
the grant of special increment for passing of Associateship of 
the Federation of Insurance Inylitute Examination , namely 
Licenciate , Associateship and Fellowship . The qualification 
stipulated for the special increment is that they should have 
Sat for the examination before the expiry of time limit pres 
cribed . The employees will be cligible for grant of special 
increment only if he passed the whole examination beforc the 
lime limit (i. e . ) 5 years of service even though the results 
are announced luter . It is also common ground that the 
Federation of Insurance Institute fixed the date for 1975 
examination and the papers for which the Petitioner , sat 
fell on 23rd October , 1975 and the Petitioners completed five 
years on 22nd October , 1975, Ex , M - 1 is the Examination 
Handbook of the Federation of Insurance Institute for the 
Examination to be held in 1975 and Licentiate Examination 
in April , 1976 . Ex . M - 1 contains Time Table for October, 
1975 Examination and the duration of the Examination was 
from Monday , the 20th October , 1975 to Sunday , the 26th 
October, 1975. On cach day , there was examination on 
different subjects. The subject9 for which the Petitioners 
appeared fell on Thursday , the 23rd October , 1975. The 
Management denied increment on the ground that the Exami 
nation fell on 23rd October , 1975 instead of 22nd October , 
1975 on which date they completed 5 years of service . It 
must be remembered that the programme for examination is 
drawn up by Federation of Insurance Institutes not by Life 
Insurance Corporation and it was beyond the powers or the 
jurisdiction of the candidates appearing for the examination , 
In these circumstances , one has to look into it as to when 
the examination commenced . Admittedly , the examination 
commenced on 20th October , 1975 . It is well within the 
period of five years . The examination for which the Peti 
tioncry appeared was only in continuation of the examination 
comincnced on 20th October , 1975 . If the examination for 
which the Petitioners appeared fell on 23rd October , 1975 
the blame cannot be attributed to the Petitionery as it is 
beyond their control, Ex , W - 2 is a Circular of the Life Insu 
tance Corporation of India . Clause (3 ) of Ex . W - 2 prescribes 
tiine limit which stipulates that an employce will not be 
entitled to special increment only if hc passes the examination 
within five years from the date of his joining his service fri 
the Corporation . Now the Management relies on sub - clause 
( ii ) of Clause ( 3 ) of Ex . W - 2 which lays down that " an 
employee should not have completed five years or 10 years 
service as the case may be , as on the first day of the com 
mencement of the examination on the passing of which ho 
becomes eligible to special increment / s " . Now the Tribunal 
has to interpret the above clause in the spirit in which it 
hay been introduced . It is significant to note that the clause 
stipulates that an employee should not have completed ſve 
years on the first day of the commencement of the exami 
nation and it does not state that on the commencement of 
his examination . Therefore what is contemplated is the coni 
mencement of the examination which has to be taken into

account as a criterion. In the present case , admittedly, the 

मतः अब , उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 10क की उपधारा ( 3 ) के 
examination was commenced on 20th October, 1973 , though 
he sat for his examination on 23rd October , 1975 which is a 

अनुसरण में , केन्द्रीय सरकार उक्त करार को , जो उसे 5 अगस्त , 1978 
continuation of the series of the examination commenced on को मिला , एतद्वारा प्रकाशित करती है । 
20th October , 1975. Therefore , I feel that having regard to 
the spirit of the above Clause 3 (b ) sub - clause (ii ), the critcrion 
is the date of commencement of the examinalion and not 

करार 
the date of the subject on which one writes. It is a continuous 
process and as such it is not proper 10 separate . Thus we find 

( औद्योगिक विवाद प्रधिनियम , 1947 की धारा 10क के अधीन ) 
that two interpretations are possible under the clause 3 (b ) 
which prescribes tinic Jimit. When two interpretations are नियोजकों का प्रतिनिधित्व करने वाले : मैसर्स तुलसीवास खिमजी प्रा . 
possible , it is the duty of the Court to interpret what is favour 
able to workmen , After all in this case , the examination had 

लिमिटेड , वीर नारिमन रोड , 
taken place on 23rd October , 1975 instead of 22nd October , 

बम्बई-400001 के लिए नियुक्त 
1975 which was beyond the Petitioners control and jurisdiction 
and as such it should be liberally construed in favour of 

किए गए पाटर्नी श्री पान्तु 
workmen , Viewed in that light, I hold that the Petitioners 
had passed the examination within the time limit prescribed 

करसोनदास । 
and as such they are entitled to special increments,

( 6 ) In the result , an Award is passed holding that Thiruvar 
largal Ganapathy and Iyyemperumal, Assistants are entitled 

परिवहन तथा गोदी श्रमिक 
to special increments ,

PART II — SEC . 3 ( ii )]

All India Workers Federation which is an All India Body 
representing Class IV employeess of the Bank , had dis 
cussions with the Central Office of the Bank at Bombay 
when the Secretary , and Vice President of the Federation 
met the Chief Manager on 25 - 7 - 1973 and supported appoint 
ment of Pete to the post of Electrician -cum - Caretaker. 
It may well be that the appointment of Shri Pete was 
sponsored by the Local Union as also All India Federation ; 
but, in my opinion that is neither bere nor there . The 
Management cannot abdicato its managerial functions in 
such manner . An appointment has to be made after 
examining the claims of all the affected employees. It is 
often said that justice should not only be done but it 
should also appear to be done. The 88mc rule would 
apply to the selection and appointment on promotion of a 
particular candidate . Unfortunately that is not followed in 
this case . 


14 . As I have pointed out the designation of the post is 
Electrician - cum -Caretaker . It is an admitted fact that at the 
time of his promotion Shri Pete did not possess the Wire 
man s Licence . Now the stand taken by the Management 
is that the duties of the Electrician - cum - Caretaker attached 
to the Amravati Road Staff Quarters are predominantly 
caretaking duties and more important than the technical 
duties of an Electrician and in view of the previous experi 
ence of Shri Pete as an Asstt . Caretaker he was selected 
for the new post . It is submitted that such appointments 
have been made by the Management even though the 
candidate did not possess the Wireman s Licence. Such 
instances are given in para 10 of the written statement. In 
my opinion , nothing turns on this aspect of the matter . In 
view of the technical qualifications prescribed in the 
Annexure to Exh . C - 3 , the Management should not have 
appointed Shri Pete to this post when he did not hold i 
Wireman s licence Certificate, especially when there were 
some 8 candidates listed in the Chart annexed to the 
written statement filed by the Rashtriya Reserve Bank 
Karmachari Sangh , Nagpur possessing such technical quali 
fications , were available for consideration . In this connection 
I may refer to the evidence as given by Shri Raju who 
is working as Air Conditioned Plant Attendant. He holds 
a certificate of competency and Wireman s Permit issued 
by the former Government of C . P . & Berar . The Govern 
mont of Maharashtra has also issued a Certificate of 
competency to him . He has undergone training and gained 


experience in the Khaparkheda Power House . He says 
that 2 – 3 years training and experience is required for 
passing this Examination and acquiring a Certificate . Ho 
says that along with him , there are 7 other worker 
holding technical qualifications like him from the beginning, 
Along with others , this witness had also made a representa 
tion to the Management to consider his claim for this 
post but, his claim was overlooked . 

15 . It is the case of the aggrieved workers that this 
appointment was offered to Sbri Pete out of turn for the 
Teason that he was a favourite of the Manager . It has come 
on record that before joining this appointment Shri Pete 
was working as a Peon to the Manager who it is sugges 
tad , might have developed a soft corner for him by 
reason of close contact. It is further pointed out that as 
per tho appointment order Exh . C - 5 , Shri Pete was given 
six months time to acquire Wireman s licence . He failed 
to acquire the licence within that period . It is submitted 
that the Management was gencrous enough to extend the 
period for obtaining Wireman s Licence for further six 
months vide Exh . C - 7 . On this background and having 
regard to the facts and circumstances of the case , the 
suggestion made by the aggrieved workers that a special 
favour has been shown to Shri Pete by the Management, 
cannot be regarded as wholly unjustified . 

16 . It would be clear from the above discussion , that 
the Management of the Reserve Bank of India , Nagpur , 
was not justified in selecting Shri Pete for the post of 
Electrician - cum -Caretaker without notifying the vacancy and 
inviting applications from amongst the Class IV Staff for 
filling in the said post. I hold accordingly . I further direct 
tho Management to notify the said post and allow all 
eligible persons from Class IV to apply for the said post 
and then make a selection on the basis of senority , edu 
cational and technical qualifications , experience , skill and 
suitability . I make an Award accordingly . 
Nagpur, Dated 26th July , 1978 . 

W . K . ALMELKAR , Presiding Officer .
